#  Chronicle API Stay organized with collections  Save and categorize content based on your preferences.

## Service: chronicle.googleapis.com

### Service endpoint

A [service endpoint](https://cloud.google.com/apis/design/glossary#api_service_endpoint) is a base URL that specifies the network address of an API service. One service might have multiple service endpoints.This service has the following service endpoints and all URIs below are relative to these service endpoints:

  * `https://us-chronicle.googleapis.com`
  * `https://eu-chronicle.googleapis.com`
  * `https://europe-chronicle.googleapis.com`

Gets a single Integration by its resource name.

`POST /upload/v1beta/{parent}/integrations:import`

`POST /upload/v1beta/{parent}/integrations:extractIntegrationDetails`

## REST Resource: [v1alpha.projects.locations.instances.legacySoarIdpMappingGroups](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1alpha/projects.locations.instances.legacySoarIdpMappingGroups)

Methods  
---

Updates multiple IDP mapping groups in a single batch operation, allowing for efficient management of large numbers of mappings.  
`[create](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1alpha/projects.locations.instances.legacySoarIdpMappingGroups/create)` |  `POST /v1alpha/{parent}/legacySoarIdpMappingGroups`

Deletes an IDP mapping group, removing the mapping between the external IdP group and SecOps resources.

`[fetchRestrictedAgents](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/fetchRestrictedAgents)` |  `GET /v1/{name}:fetchRestrictedAgents`   
Identifies remote agents that would be restricted from running an updated version of the integration, typically due to environment incompatibilities like unsupported Python versions.  
`[get](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/get)` |  `GET /v1/{name}`   
Gets a single Integration by its resource name.  
`[getFetchProductionDiff](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/getFetchProductionDiff)` |  `GET /v1/{name}:fetchProductionDiff`   
Returns the difference between the staging integration and its matching production version.  
`[getFetchStagingDiff](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/getFetchStagingDiff)` |  `GET /v1/{name}:fetchStagingDiff`   
Returns the difference between the production integration and its corresponding staging version.  
`[import](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/import)` |  `POST /v1/{parent}/integrations:import`   
`POST /upload/v1/{parent}/integrations:import`   
Imports a complete integration package from a ZIP file (up to 500MB).  
`[importIntegrationDependency](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/importIntegrationDependency)` |  `POST /v1/{name}:uploadDependency`   
`POST /upload/v1/{name}:uploadDependency`   
Uploads a raw dependency file (e.g., a wheel file or binary) to an existing custom integration.  
`[importIntegrationItems](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/importIntegrationItems)` |  `POST /v1/{name}:importItems`   
`POST /upload/v1/{name}:importItems`   
Imports individual integration items (actions, jobs, connectors, etc.) from a ZIP file into an existing custom integration.  
`[list](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/list)` |  `GET /v1/{parent}/integrations`   
Lists all Integrations installed in the instance.  
`[pushToProduction](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/pushToProduction)` |  `POST /v1/{name}:pushToProduction`   
Transitions an integration from staging to production mode.  
`[pushToStaging](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/pushToStaging)` |  `POST /v1/{name}:pushToStaging`   
Transitions an integration from production back to staging mode.  
`[updateCustomIntegration](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/updateCustomIntegration)` |  `POST /v1/{updateCustomIntegrationPayload.integration.name}:updateCustomIntegration`   
Updates a custom integration definition, including its parameters and dependencies.  
`[upload](https://docs.cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.instances.integrations/upload)` |  `POST /v1/{parent}/integrations:extractIntegrationDetails`   
`POST /upload/v1/{parent}/integrations:extractIntegrationDetails`   
Parses an integration ZIP file and returns its constituent items and metadata without importing it.
